Launched in September 2016, iPhone 7 is a smartphone designed by Apple, the successor to the iPhone 6s model. The phone is equipped with a 4.7-inch IPS LCD screen with 1134 x 750p HD resolution and 3D touch. A key feature of this device is the presence of a central "fake" glass key that uses the 3D touch technology to simulate the effect of a real physical key. The phone shell is made of aluminium and is available in six colours: matt black, glossy black, gold, pink gold, silver and red.
iPhone 7 features an Apple A10 Fusion quad core processor, capable of working at 2.23 GHz, combined with a PowerVR 7XT Plus graphics processor. Unlike previous models, this smartphone is only available in versions with 32 and 128 GB of internal memory, again not expandable, as in the iPhone SE. The installed software is iOS 10 upgradeable.
iPhone 7 can take pictures and record videos in 4K resolution, thanks to the 12 megapixel rear camera. The front camera, on the other hand, has 7 megapixels. The smartphone has a 1960 mAh battery and is compatible with 4G LTE up to 450 Mbps, Wi-Fi 802.11 a/b/g/n/ac, Bluetooth 4.2, GPS and NFC.
The product is guaranteed for 12 months. You can add an additional 12 months of warranty at the time of purchase by checking the corresponding box.

The phone is equipped with the same 4.7-inch IPS LCD technology screen, with 1330 x 740p resolution and 326 ppi density. The rear body is made of durable aluminium.
All iPhone 6s features are managed by the Apple A9 dual-core processor, capable of operating at 1.84 GHz, along with a PowerVR GT7600 graphics card. The phone is available with 2 GB of RAM and 16 or 64 GB of non-expandable internal memory, which is also available in the larger iPhone 6s Plus version. The installed software is iOS 9.
iPhone 6s is equipped with a 12 megapixel rear camera with two-tone led flash and f/2.2 focal length, capable of recording video in 4K resolution. The front camera is a 5-megapixel camera, perfect for shooting selfie and using FaceTime. The smartphone has a 1715 mAh battery and is compatible with LTE up to 300 Mbps, Wi-Fi type 802.11, Bluetooth 4.1, GPS and NFC.

The product is guaranteed for 12 months. You can add an additional 12 months of warranty at the time of purchase by checking the corresponding box." itemprop="description"/>

Xiaomi Mi A2 includes a Qualcomm Snapdragon 660 processor with a speed of 2.2 GHz, higher than other devices produced by this company such as the Xiaomi Redmi Note 5A. This device is sold in three versions with 32, 64 or 128 GB memory. The models differ in the presence of a 4 GB RAM in the 32 and 64 GB versions, while there is a 6 GB RAM in the 128 GB version.
Xiaomi Mi A2 is equipped with a double rear camera with 12 and 20 megapixels, which can be used to improve photos in low light conditions or to achieve blur effects. There is also a 20-megapixel camera on the front. Inside the device you can find a non-removable 3010 mAh battery with a fast charging system. The phone is compatible with LTE up to 600 Mbps, Wi-Fi 802.11, Bluetooth 5.0, infrared and GPS.
Full support for the 20 4G LTE band (800MHz) and a multi-language ROM with pre-installed G-Apps make the smartphone ready to use." itemprop="description"/>

<meta content="Vivo NEX Dual Display is a smartphone presented in December 2018 by the Chinese company Vivo, with an important novelty compared to its predecessor Vivo NEX S. In fact, this device has a main display with a diagonal of 6.39 inches and Super Amoled technology, a resolution Full HD + 2340 x 1080p and density of 403 ppi. A second display is also on the back of the device, with a size of 5.49 inches, Super Amoled technology and Full HD resolution 1920 x 1080p. The heart of the Vivo NEX Dual Display is a Qualcomm Snapdragon 845 octa core processor capable of operating at 2.8 GHz, along with an Adreno 630 graphics processor. As for internal memory, the model is available with 128 GB non-expandable, accompanied by 10 GB of RAM. As for the photographic features, the Vivo NEX Dual Display features a 12-megapixel rear camera accompanied by a second 2-megapixel sensor and a third 3D TOF sensor. There is no front camera, as the rear camera can be used for selfies thanks to the secondary display. The device is completed by a fingerprint sensor integrated under the glass of the screen, a 3500 mAh battery and compatibility with LTE, Wi-Fi 802.11, Bluetooth 5.0 and GPS." itemprop="description"/>

<meta content="On sale since October 2018, Xiaomi Mi MIX 3 is the successor to the Xiaomi Mi MIX 2S model, another prominent device made by the Chinese company Xiaomi. Unlike the same, this device has a 6.39-inch screen with 1080 x 2160p (FHD+) resolution.
Inside the Xiaomi Mi MIX 3 there is a Qualcomm Snapdragon 845 processor operating at 64 bit and 2.8 GHz, together with an Adreno 630 graphic processor. The device is available in the version with 128 GB of internal memory, characterized by 6 GB of RAM. It should be noted that the internal memory is not expandable unlike other models, such as the smartphone Xiaomi Redmi 5A. The installed software is Android version 9.0.
Remarkable features are also included in the photo compartment of the Xiaomi Mi MIX 3, as this device includes a 12+12 megapixel rear camera. Also present 24 + 2 megapixel front camera to shoot beautiful selfies. To complete the device a 3200 mAh battery and compatibility with Bluetooth 5.0, Wi-Fi 802.11 and GPS.

Full support for the 20 4G LTE band (800MHz) and a multi-language ROM with pre-installed G-Apps make the smartphone ready to use." itemprop="description"/>

                var href = this.href;
                var product = Appmate.wk.getItem(id).then(function(product){
                    var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
                    sendTracking({
                        hitType: 'event',
                        eventCategory: 'Product',
                        eventAction: 'click',
                        eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
                        hitCallback: function() {
                            document.location = href;
                        }
                    }, {
                        id: variant.sku || variant.id,
                        name: product.title,
                        category: product.type,
                        brand: product.vendor,
                        variant: variant.title,
                        price: variant.price,
                    }, {
                        list: "" || undefined
                    });
                })
            }),
            'click [data-wk-add-product]': eventHandler(function(){
                var productId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-add-product');
                var select = Appmate.wk.$(this).parents('form').find('select[name="id"]');
                var variantId = select.length ? select.val() : undefined;
                Appmate.wk.addProduct(productId, variantId).then(function(product) {
                    var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
                    sendTracking({
                        hitType: 'event',
                        eventCategory: 'Product',
                        eventAction: 'add-to-wishlist',
                        eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
                    }, {
                        id: variant.sku || variant.id,
                        name: product.title,
                        category: product.type,
                        brand: product.vendor,
                        variant: variant.title,
                        price: variant.price,
                    }, {
                        list: "" || undefined
                    });
                });
            }),
            'click [data-wk-remove-product]': eventHandler(function(){
                var productId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-remove-product');
                Appmate.wk.removeProduct(productId).then(function(product) {
                    var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
                    sendTracking({
                        hitType: 'event',
                        eventCategory: 'Product',
                        eventAction: 'remove-from-wishlist',
                        eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
                    }, {
                        id: variant.sku || variant.id,
                        name: product.title,
                        category: product.type,
                        brand: product.vendor,
                        variant: variant.title,
                        price: variant.price,
                    }, {
                        list: "" || undefined
                    });
                });

            }),
            'click [data-wk-remove-item]': eventHandler(function(){
                var itemId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-remove-item');
                fadeOrNot(this, function() {
                    Appmate.wk.removeItem(itemId).then(function(product){
                        var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
                        sendTracking({
                            hitType: 'event',
                            eventCategory: 'Product',
                            eventAction: 'remove-from-wishlist',
                            eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
                        }, {
                            id: variant.sku || variant.id,
                            name: product.title,
                            category: product.type,
                            brand: product.vendor,
                            variant: variant.title,
                            price: variant.price,
                        }, {
                            list: "" || undefined
                        });
                    });
                });

                setTimeout(function(){
                    if ($('body#wishlist select.wk-option-select').length) {
                        $('body#wishlist select.wk-option-select').selectric();
                    }
                }, 500);

            }),
            'click [data-wk-clear-wishlist]': eventHandler(function(){
                var wishlistId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-clear-wishlist');
                Appmate.wk.clear(wishlistId).then(function() {
                    sendTracking({
                        hitType: 'event',
                        eventCategory: 'Wishlist',
                        eventAction: 'clear-wishlist',
                        eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
                    });
                });
            }),
            'click [data-wk-share]': eventHandler(function(){
                var params = $(this).data();
                var shareLink = Appmate.wk.getShareLink(params);

                sendTracking({
                    hitType: 'event',
                    eventCategory: 'Wishlist',
                    eventAction: 'share-wishlist-' + params.wkShareService,
                    eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
                });

                switch (params.wkShareService) {
                    case 'link':
                        Appmate.wk.$('#wk-share-link-text').addClass('wk-visible').find('.wk-text').html(shareLink);
                        var copyButton = Appmate.wk.$('#wk-share-link-text .wk-clipboard');
                        var copyText = copyButton.html();
                        var clipboard = Appmate.wk.clipboard(copyButton[0]);
                        clipboard.on('success', function(e) {
                            copyButton.html('copied');
                            setTimeout(function(){
                                copyButton.html(copyText);
                            }, 2000);
                        });
                        break;
                    case 'email':
                        window.location.href = shareLink;
                        break;
                    case 'contact':
                        var contactUrl = '/pages/contact';
                        var message = 'Here is the link to my list:\n' + shareLink;
                        window.location.href=contactUrl + '?message=' + encodeURIComponent(message);
                        break;
                    default:
                        window.open(
                            shareLink,
                            'wishlist_share',
                            'height=590, width=770, toolbar=no, menubar=no, scrollbars=no, resizable=no, location=no, directories=no, status=no'
                        );
                }
            }),
            'click [data-wk-move-to-cart]': eventHandler(function(){
                var $elem = Appmate.wk.$(this);
                $form = $elem.parents('form');
                $elem.prop("disabled", true);

                var item = this.getAttribute('data-wk-move-to-cart');
                var formData = $form.serialize();

                fadeOrNot(this, function(){
                    Appmate.wk
                        .moveToCart(item, formData)
                        .then(function(product){
                            $elem.prop("disabled", false);

                            var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
                            sendTracking({
                                hitType: 'event',
                                eventCategory: 'Product',
                                eventAction: 'from-wishlist-to-cart',
                                eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
                                hitCallback: function() {
                                    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
                                    location.reload();
                                }
                            }, {
                                id: variant.sku || variant.id,
                                name: product.title,
                                category: product.type,
                                brand: product.vendor,
                                variant: variant.title,
                                price: variant.price,
                            }, {
                                list: "" || undefined
                            });
                        });
                });
            }),
            'render .wk-option-select': function(elem){
                appmateOptionSelect(elem);

            },
            'login-required': function (intent) {
                var redirect = {
                    path: window.location.pathname,
                    intent: intent
                };
                window.location = '/account/login?wk-redirect=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(redirect));
            },
            'render .swiper-container': function (elem) {
                // initSlider()
            },
            'ready': function () {
                // Appmate.wk.$(document).ready(initSlider);
                fillContactForm();


                var redirect = Appmate.wk.getQueryParam('wk-redirect');
                if (redirect) {
                    Appmate.wk.addLoginRedirect(Appmate.wk.$('#customer_login'), JSON.parse(redirect));
                }
                var intent = Appmate.wk.getQueryParam('wk-intent');
                if (intent) {
                    intent = JSON.parse(intent);
                    switch (intent.action) {
                        case 'addProduct':
                            Appmate.wk.addProduct(intent.product, intent.variant)
                            break;
                        default:
                            console.warn('Wishlist King: Intent not implemented', intent)
                    }
                    if (window.history && window.history.pushState) {
                        history.pushState(null, null, window.location.pathname);
                    }
                }

            }
        });



        Appmate.init({
            shop: {
                domain: 'grossoshop.net'
            },
            session: {
                customer: ''
            },
            globals: {
                settings: {
                    app_wk_icon: 'heart',
                    app_wk_share_position: 'top',
                    app_wk_products_per_row: 4
                },
                customer: '',
                customer_accounts_enabled: true,
            },
            wishlist: {
                loginRequired: false,
                handle: "/pages/wishlist",
                proxyHandle: "/a/wk",
                share: {
                    handle: "/pages/shared-wishlist",
                    title: 'My Wishlist',
                    description: 'Check out some of my favorite things @ GrossoShop.',
                    imageTitle: 'GrossoShop',
                    hashTags: 'wishlist',
                    twitterUser: false // will be used with via
                }
            }
        });

        function sendTracking(event, product, action) {
            if (typeof ga === 'function') {
                if (product) {
                    ga('ec:addProduct', product);
                }

                if (action) {
                    ga('ec:setAction', 'click', action);
                }

                if (event) {
                    ga('send', event);
                }

                if (!ga.loaded) {
                    event && event.hitCallback && event.hitCallback()
                }
            } else {
                event && event.hitCallback && event.hitCallback()
            }
        }

        function initSlider () {
            if (typeof Swiper !== 'undefined') {
                var mySwiper = new Swiper ('.swiper-container', {
                    direction: 'horizontal',
                    loop: false,
                    pagination: '.swiper-pagination',
                    nextButton: '.swiper-button-next',
                    prevButton: '.swiper-button-prev',
                    spaceBetween: 20,
                    slidesPerView: 4,
                    breakpoints: {
                        380: {
                            slidesPerView: 1,
                            spaceBetween: 0,
                        },
                        480: {
                            slidesPerView: 2,
                            spaceBetween: 20,
                        }
                    }
                })
            }
        }

        function fillContactForm() {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fillContactForm)
            if (document.getElementById('contact_form')) {
                var message = Appmate.wk.getQueryParam('message');
                var bodyElement = document.querySelector("#contact_form textarea[name='contact[body]']");
                if (bodyElement && message) {
                    bodyElement.value = message;
                }
            }
        }

        function eventHandler (callback) {
            return function (event) {
                if (typeof callback === 'function') {
                    callback.call(this, event)
                }
                if (event) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    event.stopPropagation();
                }
            }
        }

        function fadeOrNot(elem, callback){
            var $elem = $(elem);
            if ($elem.parents('.wk-fadeout').length) {
                $elem.parents('.wk-fadeout').fadeOut(callback);
            } else {
                callback();
            }
        }

    }
</script>

<script>
    function appmateSelectCallback(variant, selector) {
        var shop = Appmate.wk.globals.shop;
        var product = Appmate.wk.getProduct(variant.product_id);
        var itemId = Appmate.wk.$(selector.variantIdField).parents('[data-wk-item]').attr('data-wk-item');
        var container = Appmate.wk.$('#wk-item-' + itemId);


        

        var imageUrl = '';

        if (variant && variant.image) {
            imageUrl = Appmate.wk.filters.img_url(variant, '300x');
        } else if (product) {
            imageUrl = Appmate.wk.filters.img_url(product, '300x');
        }

        if (imageUrl) {
            container.find('.wk-variant-image').attr('src', imageUrl);
        }
        var formatMoney = Appmate.wk.filters.money;
        if (!product.hidden && variant && (variant.available || variant.inventory_policy === 'continue')) {
            container.find('.wk-add-to-cart').removeAttr('disabled').removeClass('disabled').attr('value', 'Aggiungi al carrello');
            if(variant.price < variant.compare_at_price){
                container.find('.wk-price-preview').html("<s>" + formatMoney(variant.compare_at_price) + "</s>" + '<span data-product-price class="product-item__price-origin product-item__price-origin--active">' + formatMoney(variant.price) + "</span>");
            } else {
                container.find('.wk-price-preview').html('<span data-product-price class="product-item__price-origin">' + formatMoney(variant.price) + "</span>");
            }
        } else {
            var message = variant && !product.hidden ? "Esaurito" : "Non disponibile";
            container.find('.wk-add-to-cart').addClass('disabled').attr('disabled', 'disabled').attr('value', message);
            container.find('.wk-price-preview').text(message).addClass('disabled');
        }

    };

    function appmateOptionSelect(el){
        if (!Shopify || !Shopify.OptionSelectors) {
            throw new Error('Missing option_selection.js! Please check templates/page.wishlist.liquid');
        }

        var id = el.getAttribute('id');
        var itemId = Appmate.wk.$(el).parents('[data-wk-item]').attr('data-wk-item');
        var container = Appmate.wk.$('#wk-item-' + itemId);

        Appmate.wk.getItem(itemId).then(function(product){
            var selector = new Shopify.OptionSelectors(id, {
                product: product,
                onVariantSelected: appmateSelectCallback,
                enableHistoryState: false
            });

            if (product.selected_variant_id) {
                selector.selectVariant(product.selected_variant_id);
            }

            // Add label if only one product option and it isn't 'Title'.
            if (product.options.length == 1 && product.options[0] != 'Title') {
                Appmate.wk.$(container)
                    .find('.selector-wrapper:first-child')
                    .prepend('<label>' + product.options[0] + '</label>');
            }

        });

    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        setTimeout(function(){
            if ($('body#wishlist select.wk-option-select').length) {
                $('body#wishlist select.wk-option-select').selectric();
            }
        }, 500);
    })

</script>
